						function reloadLocations() {
							var locations_in_use = 0; 
							var chosen_locations_div = document.getElementById('chosen_locations_div');
							var category = document.getElementById('location').value;
							if((category === '1' || category === '3' || category === '33') && (locations_in_use !== 0)){
								chosen_locations_div.classList.remove("disabled");
							} else {
								chosen_locations_div.classList.add("disabled");
							}
						};
					</script>

                <td class="block_content" colspan="2">
                  <table class="table_default">
                    <tr>
                      <td>
                      <div style="float: right"></div>						<div class="help_block"><a href="help.php?catoid=49" onClick="acalogPopup('help.php?catoid=49', 'help', 770, 530, 'yes');return false;" accesskey="h" target="_blank" class="help"><strong>HELP</strong></a></div>
						<span class="acalog_catalog_name">2026-2027 Undergraduate Catalog</span>                        <h1 id="acalog-content">2026-2027 Undergraduate Catalog</h1></td>
                    </tr>
                    <tr>
                      <td>
                        <hr>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  </table>
				  <p style="text-align:center"><img alt="" src="/mime/media/49/5112/MTSU_BalloonArch.jpg" style="height:100%; width:100%" /></p>

<p><em>Founded in 1911, Middle Tennessee State University was the oldest and largest member of the Tennessee Board of Regents system until it was granted its own governing board in 2017. It is the&#160;No. 1 producer of graduates for the Greater Nashville economy;&#160;a top choice for&#160;first-generation&#160;and&#160;transfer&#160;students, a leader in&#160;adult degree completion&#160;programs; and home to the&#160;Charlie and Hazel Daniels Veterans and Military Family Center, one of the largest and most comprehensive facilities of its kind on a college campus. MTSU also provides graduate education opportunities ranging from traditional academic-focused disciplines to professional degrees.</em></p>

<p><em>Middle Tennessee State University does not discriminate against students, employees, or applicants for admission or employment on the basis of race, color, religion, creed, national origin, sex, sexual orientation, disability, age, status as a protected veteran, genetic information, or any other legally protected class with respect to all employment, programs, and activities sponsored by MTSU. The Assistant to the President for Civil Rights Compliance has been designated to handle inquiries regarding the non-discrimination policies and can be reached at Cope Administration Building 116, 1301 East Main Street, Murfreesboro, TN 37132; Christy.Sigler@mtsu.edu; or 615-898-2185. Also see <a href="http://crc.mtsu.edu">crc.mtsu.edu</a>.</em></p>

<p><em>The catalog is only available online and is updated yearly in the spring for the next academic year. It is compiled and prepared by the Office of the University Provost. If you have questions or need assistance, please contact (615) 904-8210.</em></p>

<h4>Notice</h4>

<p>The course offerings and requirements of Middle Tennessee State University are continually under examination and revision. This catalog presents the offerings and requirements in effect at the time of publication, but there is no guarantee that they will not be changed or revoked. Students are encouraged to contact the departments and program areas&#160;for current information. This catalog is not intended to state contractual terms and does not constitute a contract between the student and the institution.</p>

<p>Middle Tennessee State University reserves the right to make changes as required in course offerings, curricula, academic policies, and other rules and regulations affecting students to be effective whenever determined by the institution. These changes will govern current and formerly enrolled students. Enrollment of all students is subject to these conditions.</p>

<p>The institution does not warrant or represent that any student who completes a course or program of study will necessarily acquire any specific skills or knowledge or will be able to successfully pass or complete any specific examination for any course, degree, or license.</p>

<p><em>Homepage Image: MTSU Creative and Visual Services</em></p>

					<script>
						function reloadLocations() {
							var locations_in_use = 0; 
							var chosen_locations_div = document.getElementById('chosen_locations_div');
							var category = document.getElementById('location').value;
							if((category === '1' || category === '3' || category === '33') && (locations_in_use !== 0)){
								chosen_locations_div.classList.remove("disabled");
							} else {
								chosen_locations_div.classList.add("disabled");
							}
						};
					</script>

					<select name="location" id="location" title="Choose Search Location" onChange="reloadLocations();">
						<option value="-1">Select an option</option>
														<option value="3" selected>Courses</option>

				  <p style="text-align:center"><img alt="" src="/mime/media/49/5112/MTSU_BalloonArch.jpg" style="height:100%; width:100%" /></p>

<p><em>Founded in 1911, Middle Tennessee State University was the oldest and largest member of the Tennessee Board of Regents system until it was granted its own governing board in 2017. It is the&#160;No. 1 producer of graduates for the Greater Nashville economy;&#160;a top choice for&#160;first-generation&#160;and&#160;transfer&#160;students, a leader in&#160;adult degree completion&#160;programs; and home to the&#160;Charlie and Hazel Daniels Veterans and Military Family Center, one of the largest and most comprehensive facilities of its kind on a college campus. MTSU also provides graduate education opportunities ranging from traditional academic-focused disciplines to professional degrees.</em></p>

<p><em>Middle Tennessee State University does not discriminate against students, employees, or applicants for admission or employment on the basis of race, color, religion, creed, national origin, sex, sexual orientation, disability, age, status as a protected veteran, genetic information, or any other legally protected class with respect to all employment, programs, and activities sponsored by MTSU. The Assistant to the President for Civil Rights Compliance has been designated to handle inquiries regarding the non-discrimination policies and can be reached at Cope Administration Building 116, 1301 East Main Street, Murfreesboro, TN 37132; Christy.Sigler@mtsu.edu; or 615-898-2185. Also see <a href="http://crc.mtsu.edu">crc.mtsu.edu</a>.</em></p>

<p><em>The catalog is only available online and is updated yearly in the spring for the next academic year. It is compiled and prepared by the Office of the University Provost. If you have questions or need assistance, please contact (615) 904-8210.</em></p>

<h4>Notice</h4>

<p>The course offerings and requirements of Middle Tennessee State University are continually under examination and revision. This catalog presents the offerings and requirements in effect at the time of publication, but there is no guarantee that they will not be changed or revoked. Students are encouraged to contact the departments and program areas&#160;for current information. This catalog is not intended to state contractual terms and does not constitute a contract between the student and the institution.</p>

<p>Middle Tennessee State University reserves the right to make changes as required in course offerings, curricula, academic policies, and other rules and regulations affecting students to be effective whenever determined by the institution. These changes will govern current and formerly enrolled students. Enrollment of all students is subject to these conditions.</p>

<p>The institution does not warrant or represent that any student who completes a course or program of study will necessarily acquire any specific skills or knowledge or will be able to successfully pass or complete any specific examination for any course, degree, or license.</p>

            transform: translate3d(0, 0, 0);
}

    top: 0;
    left: 0;

    margin-right: auto;

    visibility: hidden;

    display: block;

    height: auto;

    border: 1px solid transparent;
}

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
	var sectionsmob = document.getElementsByClassName("toggle-facts-mob");
	var toggleTextmob = document.querySelector(".toggle-text-mob");
	toggleTextmob.addEventListener("click", function() {
		var x = sectionsmob[0];
		if (x.style.display === "none" || x.style.display === "") {
			x.style.display = "block";
			toggleTextmob.textContent = "Fewer Facts >";
		}
		else {
			x.style.display = "none";
			toggleTextmob.textContent = "More Facts >";
		}
	}
								  );
}
						 );
</script>

<div class="wp-block-columns max-width is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-7387b849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-column is-layout-flow wp-block-column-is-layout-flow"><p><span><span><a href="https://www.mtsu.edu/">Home</a></span> » <span><a href="https://www.mtsu.edu/program/">Program</a></span> » <span class="breadcrumb_last" aria-current="page">Business Administration, B.B.A.</span></span></p>



<h1 id="h-jet-engine-data-dynamic-field-source-meta-dynamic-field-post-meta-program-name-hide-if-empty-true-dynamic-field-custom-true-dynamic-field-format-s-jet-engine-data-dynamic-field-source-meta-dynamic-field-post-meta-concentration-name-hide-if-empty-true-dynamic-field-custom-true-dynamic-field-format-s-jet-engine-data-dynamic-field-source-meta-dynamic-field-post-meta-degree-type-hide-if-empty-true" class="wp-block-heading hdg-bdr has-text-color has-large-font-size" style="color:#1c2b59;padding-top:0px;padding-right:0px;padding-bottom:10px;padding-left:0px;font-style:normal;font-weight:900">Business Administration, B.B.A.</h1>



<div class="wp-block-buttons is-layout-flex wp-block-buttons-is-layout-flex" style="margin-top:10px;margin-bottom:40px">
<div class="wp-block-button"><a class="wp-block-button__link has-text-color has-background has-custom-font-size wp-element-button" style="border-radius:0px;color:#333333;background-color:#ffffff00;padding-top:0;padding-right:0;padding-bottom:0;padding-left:0;font-size:15px">AVAILABLE: On Ground, Online</a></div>
</div>



<p class="has-text-color wp-block-paragraph" style="color:#131010;font-size:16px"><p>Business Administration is the cornerstone of the business world – the work of managing resources and people to meet an organization's goals. MTSU's Bachelor in Business Administration degree combines courses in management, marketing, economics, finance, and business law. We offer students broad knowledge of the functional areas of a company and how they connect to one another. These topics are especially beneficial for students with small business ownership plans and for students who want to have an administrative career in financial organizations. Business Administration degree (B.B.A.) graduates earn an average starting salary of $58,000.</p>
<p>MTSU's Jennings A. Jones College of Business is one of the top 1.5% of business schools in the world.</p>
<h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
<h3 style="font-weight: 400">What is a BBA degree?</h3>
<p style="font-weight: 400">Bachelor of Business Administration</p>
<h3 style="font-weight: 400">What is a business administration degree?</h3>
<p style="font-weight: 400">Business Administration provides students with a broad foundation across core business areas including management, marketing, economics, finance, information systems, accounting, and business law.</p>
<h3 style="font-weight: 400">What can you do with a business administration degree?</h3>
<p style="font-weight: 400">Students in the Business Administration program learn to analyze problems, make informed decisions, and lead initiatives that improve organizational performance. The degree prepares them to operate across functional areas and drive growth, efficiency, and strategic impact in a wide range of industries.</p>
<h3 style="font-weight: 400">What jobs can I get with a business administration degree?</h3>
<p style="font-weight: 400">Possibilities include, but are not limited to: Account Representative, Area Manager, Business Administrator, Business Development Representative, Business Operations Coordinator, Operations Specialist, and Project Coordinator.</p>
<h3 style="font-weight: 400">How long does it take to get a business administration degree?</h3>
<p style="font-weight: 400">The degree requires 120 credit hours. Most full-time students complete the degree in four years, though it can be finished in as few as three years with summer coursework. Others may take longer depending on work and personal commitments.</p>
</p>


</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
